Every statement was captured from the flywheel read API named above it through a recording GORM
logger and explained verbatim with EXPLAIN (ANALYZE, BUFFERS), after VACUUM (ANALYZE); the seed is
analyzed before the rollup too, so the rollup plans with statistics. The clock is
fixed at the end of the seeded range: the rollups cover every closed hour, and the hour in
progress is read raw. Latency is the wall time of the whole API call — every statement plus the
Go-side merge — as the median of 7 calls after a warm-up. The rollup pass runs through a Scheduler
that has made its start-up pass, on its own handle, so its statements (primary-key reads of
job_stats_progress and an empty range delete of each rollup table) are not captured.
